Implement the following plan:

# Plan: Add Login with Fixed Credentials

## Context

The BandWacht web UI is currently completely open — no authentication. Add a single fixed login (username `oe8yml`, random hard password) to protect the tool. No user DB table needed — credentials checked against bcrypt hash, JWT token issued on login.

## Password

`Kx8$mQ2vL#nR7wYp`

## Changes

### 1. `web/backend/requirements.txt` — add auth packages

```
passlib[bcrypt]>=1.7.4
python-jose[cryptography]>=3.3.0
python-multipart>=0.0.9
```

### 2. `web/backend/config.py` — add auth settings

```python
auth_username: str = "oe8yml"
auth_password_hash: str = "<bcrypt hash>"
auth_jwt_secret: str = "<random hex>"
auth_jwt_expire_hours: int = 24
```

### 3. `web/backend/auth.py` — **new** JWT + bcrypt utilities

- `verify_password(plain, hashed)` — bcrypt verify
- `create_access_token(subject)` — sign JWT with expiry
- `get_current_user` — FastAPI `Depends()` that validates `Authorization: Bearer` header, raises 401 on failure

### 4. `web/backend/routers/auth.py` — **new** login endpoint

- `POST /auth/login` — accepts `OAuth2PasswordRequestForm`, checks username + bcrypt hash, returns `{"access_token": "...", "token_type": "bearer"}`

### 5. `web/backend/main.py` — wire auth

- Mount auth router (public)
- Add `get_current_user` dependency to all protected routers (instances, targets, events, recordings, notifications, settings)
- Protect WebSocket endpoints: extract token from `?token=` query param

### 6. `web/frontend/src/api/client.ts` — auth header + 401 handling

- Add `getToken()` / `setToken()` / `clearToken()` using localStorage
- Inject `Authorization: Bearer` in `request()`
- On 401 → clear token, trigger `onAuthError` callback
- Add `auth.login(username, password)` and `auth.logout()`

### 7. `web/frontend/src/types/index.ts` — add `TokenResponse`

### 8. `web/frontend/src/components/LoginPage.tsx` — **new** login form

Centered card with username/password fields, dark SDR theme, error message on failure.

### 9. `web/frontend/src/App.tsx` — auth gate

- Track `token` state (init from localStorage)
- No token → `<LoginPage>`, valid token → `<AppLayout>`
- Pass `onLogout` to layout

### 10. `web/frontend/src/components/layout/Header.tsx` — logout button

Add LogOut icon button that clears token.

### 11. `web/frontend/src/utils/strings.ts` — German auth strings

### 12. WebSocket connections — pass `?token=` query param

Update spectrum and events WebSocket URLs in Dashboard components.

## Files

| File | Action |
|------|--------|
| `web/backend/requirements.txt` | Add 3 packages |
| `web/backend/config.py` | Add 4 auth settings |
| `web/backend/auth.py` | **New** — auth utilities |
| `web/backend/routers/auth.py` | **New** — login endpoint |
| `web/backend/main.py` | Mount auth, protect routes + WS |
| `web/frontend/src/api/client.ts` | Auth header, 401 handling |
| `web/frontend/src/types/index.ts` | TokenResponse type |
| `web/frontend/src/components/LoginPage.tsx` | **New** — login page |
| `web/frontend/src/App.tsx` | Auth gate |
| `web/frontend/src/components/layout/Header.tsx` | Logout button |
| `web/frontend/src/utils/strings.ts` | Auth strings |
| Dashboard WS components | Token in WS URL |

## Verification

1. `GET /api/v1/instances` without token → 401
2. `POST /api/v1/auth/login` wrong password → 401
3. `POST /api/v1/auth/login` correct → token returned
4. `GET /api/v1/instances` with Bearer token → 200
5. Frontend: no token → login page shown
6. Login with `oe8yml` / `Kx8$mQ2vL#nR7wYp` → dashboard
7. Logout → back to login
8. WebSocket spectrum + events work after login

     6→
     7→class Settings(BaseSettings):
     8→    database_url: str = "sqlite+aiosqlite:///./bandwacht_web.db"
     9→    recordings_dir: str = "./recordings"
    10→    bandwacht_module_path: str = "../.."
    11→    cors_origins: str = "http://localhost:5173,http://localhost:3416"
    12→    host: str = "0.0.0.0"
    13→    port: int = 8000
    14→    debug: bool = False
    15→
    16→    # Notification backends (configured via env vars, not UI)
    17→    notify_gotify_url: str = ""
    18→    notify_gotify_token: str = ""
    19→    notify_telegram_bot_token: str = ""
    20→    notify_telegram_chat_id: str = ""
    21→    notify_ntfy_topic: str = ""
    22→    notify_ntfy_server: str = "https://ntfy.sh"
    23→    notify_webhook_url: str = ""
    24→
    25→    model_config = {"env_prefix": "BANDWACHT_"}
    26→
    27→    @property
    28→    def recordings_path(self) -> Path:
    29→        p = Path(self.recordings_dir)
    30→        p.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    31→        return p
    32→
    33→    @property
    34→    def cors_origin_list(self) -> list[str]:
    35→        return [o.strip() for o in self.cors_origins.split(",") if o.strip()]
    36→
    37→    def configured_notifications(self) -> list[dict]:
    38→        """Return list of notification backends configured via env vars."""
    39→        result = [{"backend": "console", "enabled": True, "configured": True}]
    40→        if self.notify_gotify_url and self.notify_gotify_token:

    42→        if self.notify_telegram_bot_token and self.notify_telegram_chat_id:

    46→        if self.notify_webhook_url:
    47→            result.append({"backend": "webhook", "enabled": True, "configured": True})
    48→        return result
    49→
    50→
    51→settings = Settings()
    52→

     1→"""FastAPI application entry point."""
     2→
     3→import sys
     4→from contextlib import asynccontextmanager
     5→from pathlib import Path
     6→
     7→from fastapi import FastAPI
     8→from fastapi.middleware.cors import CORSMiddleware
     9→from fastapi.staticfiles import StaticFiles
    10→
    11→from .config import settings
    12→from .database import init_db
    13→
    14→# Add bandwacht module to path
    15→sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent.parent))
    16→
    17→
    18→@asynccontextmanager
    19→async def lifespan(app: FastAPI):
    20→    await init_db()
    21→    yield
    22→    # Shutdown: stop all monitors
    23→    from .services.monitor_manager import manager
    24→    await manager.stop_all()
    25→
    26→
    27→app = FastAPI(title="BandWacht Web", version="0.1.0", lifespan=lifespan)
    28→
    29→app.add_middleware(
    30→    CORSMiddleware,
    31→    allow_origins=settings.cors_origin_list,
    32→    allow_credentials=True,
    33→    allow_methods=["*"],
    34→    allow_headers=["*"],
    35→)
    36→
    37→# Import and include routers
    38→from .routers import events, instances, notifications, recordings, settings as settings_router, targets  # noqa: E402
    39→from .services.ws_bridge import router as ws_router  # noqa: E402
    40→
    41→app.include_router(instances.router, prefix="/api/v1", tags=["instances"])
    42→app.include_router(targets.router, prefix="/api/v1", tags=["targets"])
    43→app.include_router(events.router, prefix="/api/v1", tags=["events"])
    44→app.include_router(recordings.router, prefix="/api/v1", tags=["recordings"])
    45→app.include_router(notifications.router, prefix="/api/v1", tags=["notifications"])
    46→app.include_router(settings_router.router, prefix="/api/v1", tags=["settings"])
    47→app.include_router(ws_router)
    48→
    49→
    50→@app.get("/api/v1/health")
    51→async def health():
    52→    from .services.monitor_manager import manager
    53→    running = sum(1 for t in manager._tasks.values() if not t.done())
    54→    return {"status": "ok", "version": "0.1.0", "monitors_running": running}
    55→
    56→
    57→# Serve built frontend as static files (production)
    58→frontend_dist = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent / "frontend" / "dist"
    59→if frontend_dist.is_dir():
    60→    app.mount("/", StaticFiles(directory=str(frontend_dist), html=True), name="frontend")
    61→

     1→const BASE = '/api/v1'
     2→
     3→async function request<T>(path: string, options?: RequestInit): Promise<T> {
     4→  const res = await fetch(`${BASE}${path}`, {
     5→    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json', ...options?.headers },
     6→    ...options,
     7→  })
     8→  if (!res.ok) {
     9→    const body = await res.json().catch(() => ({ detail: res.statusText }))
    10→    throw new Error(body.detail || `HTTP ${res.status}`)
    11→  }
    12→  return res.json()
    13→}
    14→
    15→// --- Instances ---
    16→
    17→export const instances = {

    19→  get: (id: number) => request<import('../types').SdrInstance>(`/instances/${id}`),
    20→  create: (data: { name: string; url: string; enabled?: boolean; desired_profile?: string | null }) =>
    21→    request<import('../types').SdrInstance>('/instances', {
    22→      method: 'POST',
    23→      body: JSON.stringify(data),
    24→    }),
    25→  update: (id: number, data: Partial<{ name: string; url: string; enabled: boolean; desired_profile: string | null }>) =>

    29→    }),
    30→  delete: (id: number) => request<{ ok: boolean }>(`/instances/${id}`, { method: 'DELETE' }),
    31→  start: (id: number) => request<{ ok: boolean }>(`/instances/${id}/start`, { method: 'POST' }),
    32→  stop: (id: number) => request<{ ok: boolean }>(`/instances/${id}/stop`, { method: 'POST' }),

     1→"""WebSocket endpoints for live FFT and event streaming."""
     2→
     3→import asyncio
     4→import json
     5→import logging
     6→
     7→from fastapi import APIRouter, WebSocket, WebSocketDisconnect
     8→
     9→from .monitor_manager import manager
    10→
    11→logger = logging.getLogger("bandwacht.web.ws")
    12→router = APIRouter()
    13→
    14→
    15→@router.websocket("/ws/spectrum/{instance_id}")
    16→async def ws_spectrum(websocket: WebSocket, instance_id: int):
    17→    """Stream live FFT data for an instance."""
    18→    await websocket.accept()
    19→    logger.info(f"WS spectrum client connected for instance {instance_id}")
    20→    queue: asyncio.Queue = asyncio.Queue(maxsize=5)
    21→    msg_count = 0
    22→
    23→    async def on_fft(fft_data: list):
    24→        try:
    25→            queue.put_nowait(fft_data)
    26→        except asyncio.QueueFull:
    27→            # Drop oldest, keep newest
    28→            try:
    29→                queue.get_nowait()
    30→            except asyncio.QueueEmpty:
    31→                pass
    32→            try:
    33→                queue.put_nowait(fft_data)
    34→            except asyncio.QueueFull:
    35→                pass
    36→
    37→    manager.subscribe_fft(instance_id, on_fft)
    38→    try:
    39→        while True:
    40→            fft_data = await queue.get()
    41→            await websocket.send_text(json.dumps(fft_data))
    42→            msg_count += 1
    43→            if msg_count == 1:
    44→                logger.info(f"WS spectrum: first FFT frame sent to client (instance {instance_id}, {len(fft_data)} bins)")
    45→    except (WebSocketDisconnect, Exception) as e:
    46→        logger.info(f"WS spectrum client disconnected for instance {instance_id} ({type(e).__name__}, sent {msg_count} frames)")
    47→    finally:
    48→        manager.unsubscribe_fft(instance_id, on_fft)
    49→
    50→
    51→@router.websocket("/ws/events")
    52→async def ws_events(websocket: WebSocket):
    53→    """Stream live detection events globally."""
    54→    await websocket.accept()
    55→    logger.info("WS events client connected")
    56→    queue: asyncio.Queue = asyncio.Queue(maxsize=100)
    57→
    58→    async def on_event(event_data: dict):
    59→        try:
    60→            queue.put_nowait(event_data)
    61→        except asyncio.QueueFull:
    62→            pass
    63→
    64→    manager.subscribe_events(on_event)
    65→    try:
    66→        while True:
    67→            event_data = await queue.get()
    68→            await websocket.send_text(json.dumps(event_data))
    69→    except (WebSocketDisconnect, Exception) as e:
    70→        logger.info(f"WS events client disconnected ({type(e).__name__})")
    71→    finally:
    72→        manager.unsubscribe_events(on_event)
    73→

    13→export default function AppLayout({ currentPage, onNavigate, children }: AppLayoutProps) {
    14→  const [sidebarOpen, setSidebarOpen] = useState(false)

    16→
    17→  const checkHealth = useCallback(async () => {
    18→    try {

    21→    } catch (_) {
    22→      setBackendConnected(false)
    23→    }
    24→  }, [])
    25→
    26→  useEffect(() => {
    27→    checkHealth()

    29→    return () => clearInterval(interval)
    30→  }, [checkHealth])

    32→  return (

    40→      )}

    44→        fixed inset-y-0 left-0 z-50 lg:static lg:z-auto
    45→        transform transition-transform duration-200
    46→        ${sidebarOpen ? 'translate-x-0' : '-translate-x-full lg:translate-x-0'}

    48→        <Sidebar

    65→          {children}
    66→        </main>

     1→import { useCallback, useEffect, useRef } from 'react'
     2→
     3→interface SpectrumDisplayProps {
     4→  fftData: number[] | null
     5→  centerFreq: number
     6→  bandwidth: number
     7→  className?: string
     8→}
     9→
    10→const GRID_COLOR = '#1f2937'
    11→const SPECTRUM_COLOR = '#00ff41'
    12→const FILL_COLOR = 'rgba(0, 255, 65, 0.08)'
    13→const LABEL_COLOR = '#9ca3af'
    14→
    15→/** Compute percentile value from sorted array */
    16→function percentile(sorted: number[], p: number): number {
    17→  const idx = (p / 100) * (sorted.length - 1)
    18→  const lo = Math.floor(idx)
    19→  const hi = Math.ceil(idx)
    20→  if (lo === hi) return sorted[lo]
    21→  return sorted[lo] + (sorted[hi] - sorted[lo]) * (idx - lo)
    22→}
    23→
    24→export default function SpectrumDisplay({ fftData, centerFreq, bandwidth, className = '' }: SpectrumDisplayProps) {
    25→  const canvasRef = useRef<HTMLCanvasElement>(null)
    26→  const prevData = useRef<number[] | null>(null)
    27→  // Smoothed display range for stable rendering
    28→  const rangeRef = useRef<{ min: number; max: number } | null>(null)
    29→
    30→  const draw = useCallback(() => {
    31→    const canvas = canvasRef.current
    32→    if (!canvas) return
    33→    const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d')
    34→    if (!ctx) return
    35→
    36→    const rect = canvas.getBoundingClientRect()
    37→    const dpr = window.devicePixelRatio || 1
    38→    canvas.width = rect.width * dpr
    39→    canvas.height = rect.height * dpr
    40→    ctx.scale(dpr, dpr)
    41→
    42→    const w = rect.width
    43→    const h = rect.height
    44→    const padding = { top: 10, right: 10, bottom: 30, left: 50 }
    45→    const plotW = w - padding.left - padding.right
    46→    const plotH = h - padding.top - padding.bottom
    47→
    48→    // Clear
    49→    ctx.fillStyle = '#0a0e17'
    50→    ctx.fillRect(0, 0, w, h)
    51→
    52→    // Smoothing between frames
    53→    const data = fftData ?? prevData.current
    54→    if (data) prevData.current = data
    55→    if (!data || data.length === 0) return
    56→
    57→    // Auto-scale: use percentiles to find stable display range
    58→    const sorted = [...data].sort((a, b) => a - b)
    59→    const p5 = percentile(sorted, 2)
    60→    const p95 = percentile(sorted, 98)
    61→    const margin = Math.max(Math.abs(p95 - p5) * 0.1, 10)
    62→
    63→    const targetMin = p5 - margin
    64→    const targetMax = p95 + margin
    65→
    66→    // Smooth transitions (exponential moving average)
    67→    const alpha = rangeRef.current ? 0.1 : 1.0
    68→    if (!rangeRef.current) {
    69→      rangeRef.current = { min: targetMin, max: targetMax }
    70→    } else {
    71→      rangeRef.current.min += (targetMin - rangeRef.current.min) * alpha
    72→      rangeRef.current.max += (targetMax - rangeRef.current.max) * alpha
    73→    }
    74→
    75→    const valMin = rangeRef.current.min
    76→    const valMax = rangeRef.current.max
    77→    const valRange = valMax - valMin || 1
    78→
    79→    // Grid lines (5 evenly spaced)
    80→    ctx.strokeStyle = GRID_COLOR
    81→    ctx.lineWidth = 0.5
    82→    const gridSteps = 5
    83→    for (let i = 0; i <= gridSteps; i++) {
    84→      const val = valMin + (valRange / gridSteps) * i
    85→      const y = padding.top + plotH * (1 - i / gridSteps)
    86→      ctx.beginPath()
    87→      ctx.moveTo(padding.left, y)
    88→      ctx.lineTo(padding.left + plotW, y)
    89→      ctx.stroke()
    90→
    91→      ctx.fillStyle = LABEL_COLOR
    92→      ctx.font = '10px monospace'
    93→      ctx.textAlign = 'right'
    94→      ctx.fillText(`${Math.round(val)}`, padding.left - 5, y + 3)
    95→    }
    96→
    97→    // Frequency labels
    98→    const startFreq = centerFreq - bandwidth / 2
    99→    const steps = 5
   100→    ctx.textAlign = 'center'
   101→    for (let i = 0; i <= steps; i++) {
   102→      const freq = startFreq + (bandwidth / steps) * i
   103→      const x = padding.left + (plotW / steps) * i
   104→      ctx.fillStyle = LABEL_COLOR
   105→      ctx.fillText(`${(freq / 1e6).toFixed(2)}`, x, h - 5)
   106→    }
   107→
   108→    // Draw spectrum
   109→    ctx.beginPath()
   110→    ctx.strokeStyle = SPECTRUM_COLOR
   111→    ctx.lineWidth = 1.5
   112→
   113→    const binStep = plotW / data.length
   114→    for (let i = 0; i < data.length; i++) {
   115→      const x = padding.left + i * binStep
   116→      const clamped = Math.max(valMin, Math.min(valMax, data[i]))
   117→      const y = padding.top + plotH * (1 - (clamped - valMin) / valRange)
   118→      if (i === 0) ctx.moveTo(x, y)
   119→      else ctx.lineTo(x, y)
   120→    }
   121→    ctx.stroke()
   122→
   123→    // Fill under curve
   124→    ctx.lineTo(padding.left + plotW, padding.top + plotH)
   125→    ctx.lineTo(padding.left, padding.top + plotH)
   126→    ctx.closePath()
   127→    ctx.fillStyle = FILL_COLOR
   128→    ctx.fill()
   129→  }, [fftData, centerFreq, bandwidth])
   130→
   131→  useEffect(() => {
   132→    draw()
   133→  }, [draw])
   134→
   135→  useEffect(() => {
   136→    const handleResize = () => draw()
   137→    window.addEventListener('resize', handleResize)
   138→    return () => window.removeEventListener('resize', handleResize)
   139→  }, [draw])
   140→
   141→  return (
   142→    <canvas
   143→      ref={canvasRef}
   144→      className={`w-full rounded-lg ${className}`}
   145→      style={{ height: '256px' }}
   146→    />
   147→  )
   148→}
   149→

     1→import { useCallback, useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react'
     2→import { Activity, Radio, AlertTriangle } from 'lucide-react'
     3→import { instances as instancesApi } from '../../api/client'
     4→import { useWebSocket } from '../../hooks/useWebSocket'
     5→import { UI } from '../../utils/strings'
     6→import SpectrumDisplay from './SpectrumDisplay'
     7→import EventFeed from './EventFeed'
     8→import InstanceStatusCard from './InstanceStatusCard'
     9→import type { SdrInstance } from '../../types'
    10→
    11→interface LiveEvent {
    12→  type: string
    13→  instance_id: number
    14→  timestamp: string
    15→  freq_hz: number
    16→  peak_db: number
    17→  target_label: string
    18→}
    19→
    20→export default function Dashboard() {
    21→  const [instances, setInstances] = useState<SdrInstance[]>([])
    22→  const [selectedId, setSelectedId] = useState<number | null>(null)
    23→  const [fftData, setFftData] = useState<number[] | null>(null)
    24→  const [liveEvents, setLiveEvents] = useState<LiveEvent[]>([])
    25→  const [runningIds, setRunningIds] = useState<number[]>([])
    26→  const selectedIdRef = useRef(selectedId)
    27→  selectedIdRef.current = selectedId
    28→
    29→  const refreshInstances = useCallback(async () => {
    30→    const data = await instancesApi.list()
    31→    setInstances(data)
    32→    const running: number[] = []
    33→    for (const inst of data) {
    34→      try {
    35→        const status = await instancesApi.status(inst.id)
    36→        if (status.running) running.push(inst.id)
    37→      } catch (_) { /* ignore */ }
    38→    }
    39→    setRunningIds(running)
    40→    if (data.length > 0 && selectedIdRef.current === null) {
    41→      setSelectedId(data[0].id)
    42→    }
    43→  }, [])
    44→
    45→  useEffect(() => { refreshInstances() }, [refreshInstances])
    46→
    47→  // Poll instance status every 3s
    48→  useEffect(() => {
    49→    const interval = setInterval(refreshInstances, 3000)
    50→    return () => clearInterval(interval)
    51→  }, [refreshInstances])
    52→
    53→  const isSelectedRunning = selectedId != null && runningIds.includes(selectedId)
    54→
    55→  // FFT WebSocket
    56→  const fftUrl = selectedId ? `/ws/spectrum/${selectedId}` : '/ws/spectrum/0'
    57→  useWebSocket({
    58→    url: fftUrl,
    59→    enabled: isSelectedRunning,
    60→    onMessage: useCallback((data: number[]) => setFftData(data), []),
    61→  })
    62→
    63→  // Events WebSocket
    64→  useWebSocket({
    65→    url: '/ws/events',
    66→    enabled: runningIds.length > 0,
    67→    onMessage: useCallback((data: LiveEvent) => {
    68→      setLiveEvents(prev => [data, ...prev].slice(0, 50))
    69→    }, []),
    70→  })
    71→
    72→  const selected = instances.find(i => i.id === selectedId)
    73→
    74→  const handleStartAndSelect = useCallback(async (id: number) => {
    75→    setSelectedId(id)
    76→    setFftData(null)
    77→    await instancesApi.start(id)
    78→    // Poll rapidly after start to pick up connection info
    79→    for (let i = 0; i < 5; i++) {
    80→      await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 1000))
    81→      await refreshInstances()
    82→    }
    83→  }, [refreshInstances])
    84→
    85→  const handleStop = useCallback(async (id: number) => {
    86→    await instancesApi.stop(id)
    87→    setFftData(null)
    88→    await refreshInstances()
    89→  }, [refreshInstances])
    90→
    91→  return (
    92→    <div className="space-y-6">
    93→      {/* Status cards */}
    94→      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-3 gap-4">
    95→        <div className="card flex items-center gap-4">
    96→          <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-lg bg-sdr-cyan/10 flex items-center justify-center">
    97→            <Radio className="w-5 h-5 text-sdr-cyan" />
    98→          </div>
    99→          <div>
   100→            <p className="text-sm text-sdr-muted">{UI.dash_instances}</p>
   101→            <p className="text-xl font-bold">{instances.length}</p>
   102→          </div>
   103→        </div>
   104→        <div className="card flex items-center gap-4">
   105→          <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-lg bg-sdr-green/10 flex items-center justify-center">
   106→            <Activity className="w-5 h-5 text-sdr-green" />
   107→          </div>
   108→          <div>
   109→            <p className="text-sm text-sdr-muted">Aktive Monitore</p>
   110→            <p className="text-xl font-bold">{runningIds.length}</p>
   111→          </div>
   112→        </div>
   113→        <div className="card flex items-center gap-4">
   114→          <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-lg bg-sdr-amber/10 flex items-center justify-center">
   115→            <AlertTriangle className="w-5 h-5 text-sdr-amber" />
   116→          </div>
   117→          <div>
   118→            <p className="text-sm text-sdr-muted">{UI.dash_events}</p>
   119→            <p className="text-xl font-bold">{liveEvents.length}</p>
   120→          </div>
   121→        </div>
   122→      </div>
   123→
   124→      {/* Instance cards */}
   125→      {instances.length > 0 && (
   126→        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-4">
   127→          {instances.map(inst => (
   128→            <InstanceStatusCard
   129→              key={inst.id}
   130→              instance={inst}
   131→              isRunning={runningIds.includes(inst.id)}
   132→              onStart={handleStartAndSelect}
   133→              onStop={handleStop}
   134→              onSelect={setSelectedId}
   135→              selected={selectedId === inst.id}
   136→            />
   137→          ))}
   138→        </div>
   139→      )}
   140→
   141→      {/* Spectrum */}
   142→      <div className="card">
   143→        <h3 className="text-sm font-medium text-sdr-muted mb-3">
   144→          {UI.dash_spectrum}
   145→          {selected && <span className="text-sdr-cyan ml-2">— {selected.name}</span>}
   146→        </h3>
   147→        {isSelectedRunning ? (
   148→          <SpectrumDisplay
   149→            fftData={fftData}

   151→            bandwidth={selected?.bandwidth ?? 0}

   155→            <p className="text-sdr-muted text-sm">

   157→            </p>
   158→          </div>
   159→        )}
   160→      </div>
   161→
   162→      {/* Live events */}
   163→      <div className="card">

   165→        <EventFeed events={liveEvents} />
   166→      </div>
   167→    </div>
   168→  )
   169→}

     6→from .. import crud

     9→
    10→router = APIRouter()
    11→
    12→
    13→@router.get("/instances", response_model=list[InstanceRead])
    14→async def list_instances(db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)):
    15→    return await crud.get_instances(db)
    16→
    17→
    18→@router.post("/instances", response_model=InstanceRead, status_code=201)
    19→async def create_instance(data: InstanceCreate, db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)):
    20→    return await crud.create_instance(db, data)
    21→
    22→
    23→@router.get("/instances/{instance_id}", response_model=InstanceRead)
    24→async def get_instance(instance_id: int, db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)):
    25→    inst = await crud.get_instance(db, instance_id)
    26→    if not inst:
    27→        raise HTTPException(404, "Instanz nicht gefunden")
    28→    return inst
    29→
    30→
    31→@router.put("/instances/{instance_id}", response_model=InstanceRead)
    32→async def update_instance(instance_id: int, data: InstanceUpdate, db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)):
    33→    inst = await crud.update_instance(db, instance_id, data)
    34→    if not inst:
    35→        raise HTTPException(404, "Instanz nicht gefunden")
    36→    return inst
    37→
    38→
    39→@router.delete("/instances/{instance_id}")
    40→async def delete_instance(instance_id: int, db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)):
    41→    if not await crud.delete_instance(db, instance_id):
    42→        raise HTTPException(404, "Instanz nicht gefunden")
    43→    return {"ok": True}
    44→
    45→
    46→@router.post("/instances/{instance_id}/start")
    47→async def start_instance(instance_id: int, db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)):
    48→    inst = await crud.get_instance(db, instance_id)
    49→    if not inst:
    50→        raise HTTPException(404, "Instanz nicht gefunden")
    51→    from ..services.monitor_manager import manager
    52→    await manager.start_instance(instance_id, db)
    53→    return {"ok": True, "message": f"Instanz '{inst.name}' gestartet"}
    54→
    55→
    56→@router.post("/instances/{instance_id}/stop")
    57→async def stop_instance(instance_id: int, db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)):
    58→    inst = await crud.get_instance(db, instance_id)
    59→    if not inst:
    60→        raise HTTPException(404, "Instanz nicht gefunden")
    61→    from ..services.monitor_manager import manager
    62→    await manager.stop_instance(instance_id, db)
    63→    return {"ok": True, "message": f"Instanz '{inst.name}' gestoppt"}
    64→
    65→
    66→@router.get("/instances/{instance_id}/status")
    67→async def instance_status(instance_id: int, db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)):
    68→    inst = await crud.get_instance(db, instance_id)
    69→    if not inst:
    70→        raise HTTPException(404, "Instanz nicht gefunden")
    71→    from ..services.monitor_manager import manager
    72→    running = manager.is_running(instance_id)
    73→    return {
    74→        "id": inst.id,
    75→        "name": inst.name,
    76→        "is_connected": inst.is_connected,
    77→        "running": running,
    78→        "center_freq": inst.center_freq,
    79→        "bandwidth": inst.bandwidth,
    80→        "fft_size": inst.fft_size,
    81→    }
    82→
    83→
    84→@router.get("/instances/{instance_id}/profiles", response_model=list[AvailableProfile])
    85→async def get_profiles(instance_id: int, db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)):
    86→    inst = await crud.get_instance(db, instance_id)
    87→    if not inst:
    88→        raise HTTPException(404, "Instanz nicht gefunden")
    89→
    90→    from ..services.monitor_manager import fetch_profiles_from_url, manager
    91→
    92→    # If monitor is running, try to read cached profiles first
    93→    monitor = manager.get_monitor(instance_id)
    94→    if monitor and monitor.available_profiles:
    95→        return monitor.available_profiles
    96→
    97→    # Otherwise make a temporary WebSocket connection
    98→    profiles = await fetch_profiles_from_url(inst.url)
    99→    return profiles
   100→

     1→import { useCallback, useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react'
     2→
     3→interface UseWebSocketOptions {
     4→  url: string
     5→  enabled?: boolean
     6→  onMessage?: (data: any) => void
     7→  reconnectInterval?: number
     8→}
     9→
    10→export function useWebSocket({ url, enabled = true, onMessage, reconnectInterval = 3000 }: UseWebSocketOptions) {
    11→  const [connected, setConnected] = useState(false)
    12→  const wsRef = useRef<WebSocket | null>(null)
    13→  const reconnectTimer = useRef<ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | undefined>(undefined)
    14→  const onMessageRef = useRef(onMessage)
    15→  onMessageRef.current = onMessage
    16→  const enabledRef = useRef(enabled)
    17→  enabledRef.current = enabled
    18→
    19→  const connect = useCallback(() => {
    20→    if (!enabledRef.current) return
    21→
    22→    // Clean up any existing connection
    23→    if (wsRef.current) {
    24→      wsRef.current.onclose = null
    25→      wsRef.current.close()
    26→      wsRef.current = null
    27→    }
    28→
    29→    const protocol = window.location.protocol === 'https:' ? 'wss:' : 'ws:'
    30→    const wsUrl = url.startsWith('ws') ? url : `${protocol}//${window.location.host}${url}`
    31→
    32→    try {
    33→      const ws = new WebSocket(wsUrl)
    34→      wsRef.current = ws
    35→
    36→      ws.onopen = () => setConnected(true)
    37→      ws.onclose = () => {
    38→        setConnected(false)
    39→        wsRef.current = null
    40→        if (enabledRef.current) {
    41→          reconnectTimer.current = setTimeout(connect, reconnectInterval)
    42→        }
    43→      }
    44→      ws.onerror = () => ws.close()
    45→      ws.onmessage = (e) => {
    46→        try {
    47→          const data = JSON.parse(e.data)
    48→          onMessageRef.current?.(data)
    49→        } catch (_) {
    50→          // ignore
    51→        }
    52→      }
    53→    } catch (_) {
    54→      if (enabledRef.current) {
    55→        reconnectTimer.current = setTimeout(connect, reconnectInterval)
    56→      }
    57→    }
    58→  }, [url, reconnectInterval])
    59→
    60→  useEffect(() => {
    61→    clearTimeout(reconnectTimer.current)
    62→    if (wsRef.current) {
    63→      wsRef.current.onclose = null
    64→      wsRef.current.close()
    65→      wsRef.current = null
    66→    }
    67→    if (enabled) {
    68→      connect()
    69→    } else {
    70→      setConnected(false)
    71→    }
    72→    return () => {
    73→      clearTimeout(reconnectTimer.current)
    74→      if (wsRef.current) {
    75→        wsRef.current.onclose = null
    76→        wsRef.current.close()
    77→        wsRef.current = null
    78→      }
    79→    }
    80→  }, [enabled, connect])
    81→
    82→  return { connected }
    83→}
    84→

But you MUST refuse to improve or augment the code. You can still analyze existing code, write reports, or answer questions about the code behavior.

{
  "file_path": "/home/achildrenmile/bandwacht/web/backend/auth.py",
  "content": "\"\"\"JWT + bcrypt authentication utilities.\"\"\"\n\nfrom datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone\n\nfrom fastapi import Depends, HTTPException, Query, WebSocket, status\nfrom fastapi.security import OAuth2PasswordBearer\nfrom jose import JWTError, jwt\nfrom passlib.hash import bcrypt\n\nfrom .config import settings\n\noauth2_scheme = OAuth2PasswordBearer(tokenUrl=\"/api/v1/auth/login\")\n\n\ndef verify_password(plain: str, hashed: str) -> bool:\n    return bcrypt.verify(plain, hashed)\n\n\ndef create_access_token(subject: str) -> str:\n    expire = datetime.now(timezone.utc) + timedelta(hours=settings.auth_jwt_expire_hours)\n    payload = {\"sub\": subject, \"exp\": expire}\n    return jwt.encode(payload, settings.auth_jwt_secret, algorithm=\"HS256\")\n\n\nasync def get_current_user(token: str = Depends(oauth2_scheme)) -> str:\n    try:\n        payload = jwt.decode(token, settings.auth_jwt_secret, algorithms=[\"HS256\"])\n        username: str | None = payload.get(\"sub\")\n        if username is None:\n            raise HTTPException(status_code=status.HTTP_401_UNAUTHORIZED, detail=\"Ungültiger Token\")\n    except JWTError:\n        raise HTTPException(status_code=status.HTTP_401_UNAUTHORIZED, detail=\"Ungültiger Token\")\n    return username\n\n\nasync def validate_ws_token(websocket: WebSocket, token: str = Query(default=\"\")) -> str:\n    \"\"\"Validate token from WebSocket query parameter.\"\"\"\n    if not token:\n        await websocket.close(code=1008)\n        raise HTTPException(status_code=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN)\n    try:\n        payload = jwt.decode(token, settings.auth_jwt_secret, algorithms=[\"HS256\"])\n        username: str | None = payload.get(\"sub\")\n        if username is None:\n            await websocket.close(code=1008)\n            raise HTTPException(status_code=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN)\n    except JWTError:\n        await websocket.close(code=1008)\n        raise HTTPException(status_code=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN)\n    return username\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/home/achildrenmile/bandwacht/web/backend/main.py",
  "content": "\"\"\"FastAPI application entry point.\"\"\"\n\nimport sys\nfrom contextlib import asynccontextmanager\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nfrom fastapi import Depends, FastAPI\nfrom fastapi.middleware.cors import CORSMiddleware\nfrom fastapi.staticfiles import StaticFiles\n\nfrom .auth import get_current_user\nfrom .config import settings\nfrom .database import init_db\n\n# Add bandwacht module to path\nsys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent.parent))\n\n\n@asynccontextmanager\nasync def lifespan(app: FastAPI):\n    await init_db()\n    yield\n    # Shutdown: stop all monitors\n    from .services.monitor_manager import manager\n    await manager.stop_all()\n\n\napp = FastAPI(title=\"BandWacht Web\", version=\"0.1.0\", lifespan=lifespan)\n\napp.add_middleware(\n    CORSMiddleware,\n    allow_origins=settings.cors_origin_list,\n    allow_credentials=True,\n    allow_methods=[\"*\"],\n    allow_headers=[\"*\"],\n)\n\n# Import and include routers\nfrom .routers import auth as auth_router  # noqa: E402\nfrom .routers import events, instances, notifications, recordings, settings as settings_router, targets  # noqa: E402\nfrom .services.ws_bridge import router as ws_router  # noqa: E402\n\n# Auth router (public)\napp.include_router(auth_router.router, prefix=\"/api/v1\", tags=[\"auth\"])\n\n# Protected routers — require valid JWT\n_auth = [Depends(get_current_user)]\napp.include_router(instances.router, prefix=\"/api/v1\", tags=[\"instances\"], dependencies=_auth)\napp.include_router(targets.router, prefix=\"/api/v1\", tags=[\"targets\"], dependencies=_auth)\napp.include_router(events.router, prefix=\"/api/v1\", tags=[\"events\"], dependencies=_auth)\napp.include_router(recordings.router, prefix=\"/api/v1\", tags=[\"recordings\"], dependencies=_auth)\napp.include_router(notifications.router, prefix=\"/api/v1\", tags=[\"notifications\"], dependencies=_auth)\napp.include_router(settings_router.router, prefix=\"/api/v1\", tags=[\"settings\"], dependencies=_auth)\napp.include_router(ws_router)\n\n\n@app.get(\"/api/v1/health\")\nasync def health():\n    from .services.monitor_manager import manager\n    running = sum(1 for t in manager._tasks.values() if not t.done())\n    return {\"status\": \"ok\", \"version\": \"0.1.0\", \"monitors_running\": running}\n\n\n# Serve built frontend as static files (production)\nfrontend_dist = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent / \"frontend\" / \"dist\"\nif frontend_dist.is_dir():\n    app.mount(\"/\", StaticFiles(directory=str(frontend_dist), html=True), name=\"frontend\")\n"
}

     1→"""WebSocket endpoints for live FFT and event streaming."""
     2→
     3→import asyncio
     4→import json
     5→import logging
     6→
     7→from fastapi import APIRouter, WebSocket, WebSocketDisconnect
     8→
     9→from .monitor_manager import manager
    10→
    11→logger = logging.getLogger("bandwacht.web.ws")
    12→router = APIRouter()
    13→
    14→
    15→@router.websocket("/ws/spectrum/{instance_id}")
    16→async def ws_spectrum(websocket: WebSocket, instance_id: int):
    17→    """Stream live FFT data for an instance."""
    18→    await websocket.accept()
    19→    logger.info(f"WS spectrum client connected for instance {instance_id}")
    20→    queue: asyncio.Queue = asyncio.Queue(maxsize=5)
    21→    msg_count = 0
    22→
    23→    async def on_fft(fft_data: list):
    24→        try:
    25→            queue.put_nowait(fft_data)
    26→        except asyncio.QueueFull:
    27→            # Drop oldest, keep newest
    28→            try:
    29→                queue.get_nowait()
    30→            except asyncio.QueueEmpty:
    31→                pass
    32→            try:
    33→                queue.put_nowait(fft_data)
    34→            except asyncio.QueueFull:
    35→                pass
    36→
    37→    manager.subscribe_fft(instance_id, on_fft)
    38→    try:
    39→        while True:
    40→            fft_data = await queue.get()
    41→            await websocket.send_text(json.dumps(fft_data))
    42→            msg_count += 1
    43→            if msg_count == 1:
    44→                logger.info(f"WS spectrum: first FFT frame sent to client (instance {instance_id}, {len(fft_data)} bins)")
    45→    except (WebSocketDisconnect, Exception) as e:
    46→        logger.info(f"WS spectrum client disconnected for instance {instance_id} ({type(e).__name__}, sent {msg_count} frames)")
    47→    finally:
    48→        manager.unsubscribe_fft(instance_id, on_fft)
    49→
    50→
    51→@router.websocket("/ws/events")
    52→async def ws_events(websocket: WebSocket):
    53→    """Stream live detection events globally."""
    54→    await websocket.accept()
    55→    logger.info("WS events client connected")
    56→    queue: asyncio.Queue = asyncio.Queue(maxsize=100)
    57→
    58→    async def on_event(event_data: dict):
    59→        try:
    60→            queue.put_nowait(event_data)
    61→        except asyncio.QueueFull:
    62→            pass
    63→
    64→    manager.subscribe_events(on_event)
    65→    try:
    66→        while True:
    67→            event_data = await queue.get()
    68→            await websocket.send_text(json.dumps(event_data))
    69→    except (WebSocketDisconnect, Exception) as e:
    70→        logger.info(f"WS events client disconnected ({type(e).__name__})")
    71→    finally:
    72→        manager.unsubscribe_events(on_event)
    73→

```json
{
  "file_path": "/home/achildrenmile/bandwacht/web/backend/services/ws_bridge.py",
  "content": "\"\"\"WebSocket endpoints for live FFT and event streaming.\"\"\"\n\nimport asyncio\nimport json\nimport logging\n\nfrom fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, WebSocket, WebSocketDisconnect\n\nfrom ..auth import validate_ws_token\nfrom .monitor_manager import manager\n\nlogger = logging.getLogger(\"bandwacht.web.ws\")\nrouter = APIRouter()\n\n\n@router.websocket(\"/ws/spectrum/{instance_id}\")\nasync def ws_spectrum(websocket: WebSocket, instance_id: int, _user: str = Depends(validate_ws_token)):\n    \"\"\"Stream live FFT data for an instance.\"\"\"\n    await websocket.accept()\n    logger.info(f\"WS spectrum client connected for instance {instance_id}\")\n    queue: asyncio.Queue = asyncio.Queue(maxsize=5)\n    msg_count = 0\n\n    async def on_fft(fft_data: list):\n        try:\n            queue.put_nowait(fft_data)\n        except asyncio.QueueFull:\n            # Drop oldest, keep newest\n            try:\n                queue.get_nowait()\n            except asyncio.QueueEmpty:\n                pass\n            try:\n                queue.put_nowait(fft_data)\n            except asyncio.QueueFull:\n                pass\n\n    manager.subscribe_fft(instance_id, on_fft)\n    try:\n        while True:\n            fft_data = await queue.get()\n            await websocket.send_text(json.dumps(fft_data))\n            msg_count += 1\n            if msg_count == 1:\n                logger.info(f\"WS spectrum: first FFT frame sent to client (instance {instance_id}, {len(fft_data)} bins)\")\n    except (WebSocketDisconnect, Exception) as e:\n        logger.info(f\"WS spectrum client disconnected for instance {instance_id} ({type(e).__name__}, sent {msg_count} frames)\")\n    finally:\n        manager.unsubscribe_fft(instance_id, on_fft)\n\n\n@router.websocket(\"/ws/events\")\nasync def ws_events(websocket: WebSocket, _user: str = Depends(validate_ws_token)):\n    \"\"\"Stream live detection events globally.\"\"\"\n    await websocket.accept()\n    logger.info(\"WS events client connected\")\n    queue: asyncio.Queue = asyncio.Queue(maxsize=100)\n\n    async def on_event(event_data: dict):\n        try:\n            queue.put_nowait(event_data)\n        except asyncio.QueueFull:\n            pass\n\n    manager.subscribe_events(on_event)\n    try:\n        while True:\n            event_data = await queue.get()\n            await websocket.send_text(json.dumps(event_data))\n    except (WebSocketDisconnect, Exception) as e:\n        logger.info(f\"WS events client disconnected ({type(e).__name__})\")\n    finally:\n        manager.unsubscribe_events(on_event)\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/home/achildrenmile/bandwacht/web/frontend/src/api/client.ts",
  "content": "const BASE = '/api/v1'\n\nconst TOKEN_KEY = 'bandwacht_token'\n\nexport function getToken(): string | null {\n  return localStorage.getItem(TOKEN_KEY)\n}\n\nexport function setToken(token: string): void {\n  localStorage.setItem(TOKEN_KEY, token)\n}\n\nexport function clearToken(): void {\n  localStorage.removeItem(TOKEN_KEY)\n}\n\nlet onAuthError: (() => void) | null = null\n\nexport function setOnAuthError(cb: () => void): void {\n  onAuthError = cb\n}\n\nasync function request<T>(path: string, options?: RequestInit): Promise<T> {\n  const headers: Record<string, string> = { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }\n  const token = getToken()\n  if (token) {\n    headers['Authorization'] = `Bearer ${token}`\n  }\n  const res = await fetch(`${BASE}${path}`, {\n    headers: { ...headers, ...options?.headers },\n    ...options,\n  })\n  if (res.status === 401) {\n    clearToken()\n    onAuthError?.()\n    throw new Error('Nicht autorisiert')\n  }\n  if (!res.ok) {\n    const body = await res.json().catch(() => ({ detail: res.statusText }))\n    throw new Error(body.detail || `HTTP ${res.status}`)\n  }\n  return res.json()\n}\n\n// --- Auth ---\n\nexport const auth = {\n  login: async (username: string, password: string): Promise<import('../types').TokenResponse> => {\n    const body = new URLSearchParams({ username, password, grant_type: 'password' })\n    const res = await fetch(`${BASE}/auth/login`, {\n      method: 'POST',\n      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/x-www-form-urlencoded' },\n      body,\n    })\n    if (!res.ok) {\n      const data = await res.json().catch(() => ({ detail: res.statusText }))\n      throw new Error(data.detail || `HTTP ${res.status}`)\n    }\n    const data: import('../types').TokenResponse = await res.json()\n    setToken(data.access_token)\n    return data\n  },\n  logout: () => {\n    clearToken()\n  },\n}\n\n// --- Instances ---\n\nexport const instances = {\n  list: () => request<import('../types').SdrInstance[]>('/instances'),\n  get: (id: number) => request<import('../types').SdrInstance>(`/instances/${id}`),\n  create: (data: { name: string; url: string; enabled?: boolean; desired_profile?: string | null }) =>\n    request<import('../types').SdrInstance>('/instances', {\n      method: 'POST',\n      body: JSON.stringify(data),\n    }),\n  update: (id: number, data: Partial<{ name: string; url: string; enabled: boolean; desired_profile: string | null }>) =>\n    request<import('../types').SdrInstance>(`/instances/${id}`, {\n      method: 'PUT',\n      body: JSON.stringify(data),\n    }),\n  delete: (id: number) => request<{ ok: boolean }>(`/instances/${id}`, { method: 'DELETE' }),\n  start: (id: number) => request<{ ok: boolean }>(`/instances/${id}/start`, { method: 'POST' }),\n  stop: (id: number) => request<{ ok: boolean }>(`/instances/${id}/stop`, { method: 'POST' }),\n  status: (id: number) => request<any>(`/instances/${id}/status`),\n  profiles: (id: number) => request<import('../types').AvailableProfile[]>(`/instances/${id}/profiles`),\n}\n\n// --- Targets ---\n\nexport const targets = {\n  list: (instanceId?: number) =>\n    request<import('../types').WatchTarget[]>(\n      `/targets${instanceId != null ? `?instance_id=${instanceId}` : ''}`\n    ),\n  create: (data: {\n    instance_id: number\n    freq_hz: number\n    bandwidth_hz?: number\n    label?: string\n    threshold_db?: number\n    enabled?: boolean\n  }) =>\n    request<import('../types').WatchTarget>('/targets', {\n      method: 'POST',\n      body: JSON.stringify(data),\n    }),\n  update: (id: number, data: Partial<import('../types').WatchTarget>) =>\n    request<import('../types').WatchTarget>(`/targets/${id}`, {\n      method: 'PUT',\n      body: JSON.stringify(data),\n    }),\n  delete: (id: number) => request<{ ok: boolean }>(`/targets/${id}`, { method: 'DELETE' }),\n}\n\n// --- Events ---\n\nexport const events = {\n  list: (params?: {\n    page?: number\n    page_size?: number\n    instance_id?: number\n    freq_min?: number\n    freq_max?: number\n    date_from?: string\n    date_to?: string\n    label?: string\n  }) => {\n    const searchParams = new URLSearchParams()\n    if (params) {\n      Object.entries(params).forEach(([k, v]) => {\n        if (v != null) searchParams.set(k, String(v))\n      })\n    }\n    const qs = searchParams.toString()\n    return request<import('../types').PaginatedResponse<import('../types').DetectionEvent>>(\n      `/events${qs ? `?${qs}` : ''}`\n    )\n  },\n  stats: () => request<import('../types').EventStats>('/events/stats'),\n  exportCsv: () => `${BASE}/events/export`,\n}\n\n// --- Recordings ---\n\nexport const recordings = {\n  list: () => request<string[]>('/recordings'),\n  url: (filename: string) => `${BASE}/recordings/${encodeURIComponent(filename)}`,\n  delete: (filename: string) =>\n    request<{ ok: boolean }>(`/recordings/${encodeURIComponent(filename)}`, { method: 'DELETE' }),\n}\n\n// --- Notifications ---\n\nexport const notifications = {\n  list: () => request<import('../types').NotificationConfig[]>('/notifications'),\n  test: (backend: string) => request<{ ok: boolean }>(`/notifications/${backend}/test`, { method: 'POST' }),\n}\n\n// --- Settings ---\n\nexport const settings = {\n  get: () => request<import('../types').GlobalSettings>('/settings'),\n  update: (data: Partial<import('../types').GlobalSettings>) =>\n    request<import('../types').GlobalSettings>('/settings', {\n      method: 'PUT',\n      body: JSON.stringify(data),\n    }),\n}\n\n// --- Health ---\n\nexport const health = () => request<{ status: string; version: string }>('/health')\n"
}

  setLoading(true)\n    try {\n      const result = await auth.login(username, password)\n      onLogin(result.access_token)\n    } catch (err: any) {\n      setError(UI.auth_error)\n    } finally {\n      setLoading(false)\n    }\n  }\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"h-screen bg-sdr-bg flex items-center justify-center p-4\">\n      <div className=\"w-full max-w-sm\">\n        <div        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className=\"card space-y-4\">\n          <h2 className=\"text-lg font-semibold text-center\">{UI.auth_login_title}</h2>\n\n          {error && (\n            <div   "content": "import { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from 'react'\nimport { getToken, setToken, clearToken, setOnAuthError } from './api/client'\nimport AppLayout from './components/layout/AppLayout'\nimport LoginPage from './components/LoginPage'\nimport Dashboard from './components/dashboard/Dashboard'\nimport ConfigPage from './components/config/ConfigPage'\nimport HistoryPage from './components/history/HistoryPage'\nimport type { Page } from './types'\n\nexport default function App() {\n  const [page, setPage] = useState<Page>('dashboard')\n  const [token, setTokenState] = useState<string | null>(getToken)\n\n  const handleLogout = useCallback(() => {\n    clearToken()\n    setTokenState(null)\n  }, [])\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    setOnAuthError(handleLogout)\n  }, [handleLogout])\n\n  const handleLogin = useCallback((newToken: string) => {\n    setToken(newToken)\n    setTokenState(newToken)\n  }, [])\n\n  if (!token) {\n    return <LoginPage onLogin={handleLogin} />\n  }\n\n  return (\n    <AppLayout currentPage={page} onNavigate={setPage} onLogout={handleLogout}>\n      {page === 'dashboard' && <Dashboard />}\n      {page === 'config' && <ConfigPage />}\n      {page === 'history' && <HistoryPage />}\n    </AppLayout>\n  )\n}\n"
}

(\n          <div className=\"flex items-center gap-1.5 text-sdr-green text-xs\">\n            <Wifi className=\"w-3.5 h-3.5\" />\n            <span>{UI.dash_connected}</span>\n          </div>\n        ) : (\n          <div className=\"flex items-center gap-1.5 text-sdr-muted text-xs\">\n            <WifiOff className=\"w-3.5 h-3.5\" />\n            <span>{UI.dash_disconnected}</span>\n          </div>\n        )}\n        <button\n          onClick={onLogout}\n          className=\"text-sdr-muted hover:text-sdr-red transition-colors\"\n          title={UI.auth_logout}\n        >\n          <LogOut className=\"w-4 h-4\" />\n        </button>\n      </div>\n    </header>\n  )\n}\n"

```json
{
  "file_path": "/home/achildrenmile/bandwacht/web/frontend/src/hooks/useWebSocket.ts",
  "content": "import { useCallback, useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react'\nimport { getToken } from '../api/client'\n\ninterface UseWebSocketOptions {\n  url: string\n  enabled?: boolean\n  onMessage?: (data: any) => void\n  reconnectInterval?: number\n}\n\nexport function useWebSocket({ url, enabled = true, onMessage, reconnectInterval = 3000 }: UseWebSocketOptions) {\n  const [connected, setConnected] = useState(false)\n  const wsRef = useRef<WebSocket | null>(null)\n  const reconnectTimer = useRef<ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | undefined>(undefined)\n  const onMessageRef = useRef(onMessage)\n  onMessageRef.current = onMessage\n  const enabledRef = useRef(enabled)\n  enabledRef.current = enabled\n\n  const connect = useCallback(() => {\n    if (!enabledRef.current) return\n\n    // Clean up any existing connection\n    if (wsRef.current) {\n      wsRef.current.onclose = null\n      wsRef.current.close()\n      wsRef.current = null\n    }\n\n    const protocol = window.location.protocol === 'https:' ? 'wss:' : 'ws:'\n    const base = url.startsWith('ws') ? url : `${protocol}//${window.location.host}${url}`\n    const token = getToken()\n    const sep = base.includes('?') ? '&' : '?'\n    const wsUrl = token ? `${base}${sep}token=${encodeURIComponent(token)}` : base\n\n    try {\n      const ws = new WebSocket(wsUrl)\n      wsRef.current = ws\n\n      ws.onopen = () => setConnected(true)\n      ws.onclose = () => {\n        setConnected(false)\n        wsRef.current = null\n        if (enabledRef.current) {\n          reconnectTimer.current = setTimeout(connect, reconnectInterval)\n        }\n      }\n      ws.onerror = () => ws.close()\n      ws.onmessage = (e) => {\n        try {\n          const data = JSON.parse(e.data)\n          onMessageRef.current?.(data)\n        } catch (_) {\n          // ignore\n        }\n      }\n    } catch (_) {\n      if (enabledRef.current) {\n        reconnectTimer.current = setTimeout(connect, reconnectInterval)\n      }\n    }\n  }, [url, reconnectInterval])\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    clearTimeout(reconnectTimer.current)\n    if (wsRef.current) {\n      wsRef.current.onclose = null\n      wsRef.current.close()\n      wsRef.current = null\n    }\n    if (enabled) {\n      connect()\n    } else {\n      setConnected(false)\n    }\n    return () => {\n      clearTimeout(reconnectTimer.current)\n      if (wsRef.current) {\n        wsRef.current.onclose = null\n        wsRef.current.close()\n        wsRef.current = null\n      }\n    }\n  }, [enabled, connect])\n\n  return { connected }\n}\n"
}

All the CSS classes used in LoginPage exist. Let me also verify the `health` endpoint - it's currently in the `request()` function which adds an auth header, but health should probably remain public. Let me check if that's an issue.

Looking at the plan again, health is called from `AppLayout` to check backend connectivity. Since after login we have a token, the health endpoint will include the auth header. But main.py doesn't add `get_current_user` dependency to the health endpoint. The health endpoint doesn't have auth protection, so it will work fine with or without a token — the `Authorization` header will just be ignored.

However, there's an issue: the health check in `AppLayout` runs while the user is logged in, so it will have a token. That's fine. But before login, the `LoginPage` doesn't call health. So no issue.

> AGENT

I see a potential issue with the `validate_ws_token` — calling `websocket.close()` before `websocket.accept()` might error. For WebSocket auth, the dependency runs before the endpoint body (where `accept()` is called). If we try to close a WS that hasn't been accepted yet, it could fail. Let me fix this to use a different approach — we should raise `WebSocketException` instead.

> TOOL

websocket.close(code=1008)\n        raise HTTPException(status_code=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN)\n    try:\n        payload = jwt.decode(token, settings.auth_jwt_secret, algorithms=[\"HS256\"])\n   "new_string": "\"\"\"JWT + bcrypt authentication utilities.\"\"\"\n\nfrom datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone\n\nfrom fastapi import Depends, HTTPException, Query, WebSocket, status\nfrom fastapi.security import OAuth2PasswordBearer\nfrom jose import JWTError, jwt\nfrom passlib.hash import bcrypt\nfrom starlette.websockets import WebSocketClose\n\nfrom .config import settings\n\noauth2_scheme = OAuth2PasswordBearer(tokenUrl=\"/api/v1/auth/login\")\n\n\ndef verify_password(plain: str, hashed: str) -> bool:\n    return bcrypt.verify(plain, hashed)\n\n\ndef create_access_token(subject: str) -> str:\n    expire = datetime.now(timezone.utc) + timedelta(hours=settings.auth_jwt_expire_hours)\n    payload = {\"sub\": subject, \"exp\": expire}\n    return jwt.encode(payload, settings.auth_jwt_secret, algorithm=\"HS256\")\n\n\nasync def get_current_user(token: str = Depends(oauth2_scheme)) -> str:\n    try:\n        payload = jwt.decode(token, settings.auth_jwt_secret, algorithms=[\"HS256\"])\n        username: str | None = payload.get(\"sub\")\n        if username is None:\n            raise HTTPException(status_code=status.HTTP_401_UNAUTHORIZED, detail=\"Ungültiger Token\")\n    except JWTError:\n        raise HTTPException(status_code=status.HTTP_401_UNAUTHORIZED, detail=\"Ungültiger Token\")\n    return username\n\n\nasync def validate_ws_token(websocket: WebSocket, token: str = Query(default=\"\")) -> str:\n    \"\"\"Validate token from WebSocket query parameter.\"\"\"\n    if not token:\n        raise WebSocketClose(code=1008, reason=\"Token fehlt\")\n    try:\n        payload = jwt.decode(token, settings.auth_jwt_secret, algorithms=[\"HS256\"])\n        username: str | None = payload.get(\"sub\")\n        if username is None:\n            raise WebSocketClose(code=1008, reason=\"Ungültiger Token\")\n    except JWTError:\n        raise WebSocketClose(code=1008, reason=\"Ungültiger Token\")\n    return username"

Wait, `WebSocketClose` may not exist as an exception in starlette. Let me check what the proper FastAPI approach is for rejecting WebSocket connections in a dependency.

The correct approach in FastAPI is to use `WebSocketException` (available since starlette 0.26+). Let me verify:

> TOOL

> AGENT

No FastAPI in this environment. But `WebSocketException` is available in starlette (and re-exported by FastAPI) since starlette 0.26. Given the requirements specify fastapi>=0.115.0, which includes starlette>=0.38, `WebSocketException` should definitely exist. However, looking at the starlette source, the proper way to deny a WS in a dependency before accept is actually simpler: we can just accept and immediately close, or use the `WebSocketException`. Let me use a well-tested pattern instead.

Actually, the safest approach is to not use a dependency at all for WebSocket auth, and instead validate the token manually inside the endpoint after `accept()`. But the plan says to use a dependency. Let me use `WebSocketException` which is the modern FastAPI approach:

> AGENT

Wait, but if `validate_ws_token` accepts the websocket and closes it, then the endpoint's `await websocket.accept()` will fail because it's already been accepted/closed. I need to adjust the ws_bridge endpoints to not call `accept()` themselves when using the dependency. Actually, the better pattern is: the dependency validates without accept/close, and the endpoint handles the lifecycle. Let me take a different approach — do the validation inside the endpoint body instead of using a dependency:

> TOOL

tool_use Edit
id: toolu_01Lo85BUC8v9kQHTZFXbHbTr
```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/home/achildrenmile/bandwacht/web/backend/auth.py",
  "old_string": "async def validate_ws_token(websocket: WebSocket, token: str = Query(default=\"\")) -> str:\n    \"\"\"Validate token from WebSocket query parameter.\n\n    Must be called before websocket.accept() — on failure we accept then\n    immediately close with a policy-violation code so the client gets feedback.\n    \"\"\"\n    if not token:\n        await websocket.accept()\n        await websocket.close(code=1008, reason=\"Token fehlt\")\n        raise HTTPException(status_code=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN)\n    try:\n        payload = jwt.decode(token, settings.auth_jwt_secret, algorithms=[\"HS256\"])\n        username: str | None = payload.get(\"sub\")\n        if username is None:\n            await websocket.accept()\n            await websocket.close(code=1008, reason=\"Ungültiger Token\")\n            raise HTTPException(status_code=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN)\n    except JWTError:\n        await websocket.accept()\n        await websocket.close(code=1008, reason=\"Ungültiger Token\")\n        raise HTTPException(status_code=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN)\n    return username",
  "new_string": "def verify_ws_token(token: str | None) -> str | None:\n    \"\"\"Validate a token string from a WebSocket query parameter.\n\n    Returns the username on success, None on failure.\n    \"\"\"\n    if not token:\n        return None\n    try:\n        payload = jwt.decode(token, settings.auth_jwt_secret, algorithms=[\"HS256\"])\n        return payload.get(\"sub\")\n    except JWTError:\n        return None"
}

{
  "file_path": "/home/achildrenmile/bandwacht/web/backend/services/ws_bridge.py",
  "content": "\"\"\"WebSocket endpoints for live FFT and event streaming.\"\"\"\n\nimport asyncio\nimport json\nimport logging\n\nfrom fastapi import APIRouter, Query, WebSocket, WebSocketDisconnect\n\nfrom ..auth import verify_ws_token\nfrom .monitor_manager import manager\n\nlogger = logging.getLogger(\"bandwacht.web.ws\")\nrouter = APIRouter()\n\n\n@router.websocket(\"/ws/spectrum/{instance_id}\")\nasync def ws_spectrum(websocket: WebSocket, instance_id: int, token: str = Query(default=\"\")):\n    \"\"\"Stream live FFT data for an instance.\"\"\"\n    user = verify_ws_token(token)\n    if not user:\n        await websocket.close(code=1008, reason=\"Nicht autorisiert\")\n        return\n\n    await websocket.accept()\n    logger.info(f\"WS spectrum client connected for instance {instance_id}\")\n    queue: asyncio.Queue = asyncio.Queue(maxsize=5)\n    msg_count = 0\n\n    async def on_fft(fft_data: list):\n        try:\n            queue.put_nowait(fft_data)\n        except asyncio.QueueFull:\n            # Drop oldest, keep newest\n            try:\n                queue.get_nowait()\n            except asyncio.QueueEmpty:\n                pass\n            try:\n                queue.put_nowait(fft_data)\n            except asyncio.QueueFull:\n                pass\n\n    manager.subscribe_fft(instance_id, on_fft)\n    try:\n        while True:\n            fft_data = await queue.get()\n            await websocket.send_text(json.dumps(fft_data))\n            msg_count += 1\n            if msg_count == 1:\n                logger.info(f\"WS spectrum: first FFT frame sent to client (instance {instance_id}, {len(fft_data)} bins)\")\n    except (WebSocketDisconnect, Exception) as e:\n        logger.info(f\"WS spectrum client disconnected for instance {instance_id} ({type(e).__name__}, sent {msg_count} frames)\")\n    finally:\n        manager.unsubscribe_fft(instance_id, on_fft)\n\n\n@router.websocket(\"/ws/events\")\nasync def ws_events(websocket: WebSocket, token: str = Query(default=\"\")):\n    \"\"\"Stream live detection events globally.\"\"\"\n    user = verify_ws_token(token)\n    if not user:\n        await websocket.close(code=1008, reason=\"Nicht autorisiert\")\n        return\n\n    await websocket.accept()\n    logger.info(\"WS events client connected\")\n    queue: asyncio.Queue = asyncio.Queue(maxsize=100)\n\n    async def on_event(event_data: dict):\n        try:\n            queue.put_nowait(event_data)\n        except asyncio.QueueFull:\n            pass\n\n    manager.subscribe_events(on_event)\n    try:\n        while True:\n            event_data = await queue.get()\n            await websocket.send_text(json.dumps(event_data))\n    except (WebSocketDisconnect, Exception) as e:\n        logger.info(f\"WS events client disconnected ({type(e).__name__})\")\n    finally:\n        manager.unsubscribe_events(on_event)\n"
}
```

     1→"""JWT + bcrypt authentication utilities."""
     2→
     3→from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone
     4→
     5→from fastapi import Depends, HTTPException, status
     6→from fastapi.security import OAuth2PasswordBearer
     7→from jose import JWTError, jwt
     8→from passlib.hash import bcrypt
     9→
    10→from .config import settings
    11→
    12→oauth2_scheme = OAuth2PasswordBearer(tokenUrl="/api/v1/auth/login")
    13→
    14→
    15→def verify_password(plain: str, hashed: str) -> bool:
    16→    return bcrypt.verify(plain, hashed)
    17→
    18→
    19→def create_access_token(subject: str) -> str:
    20→    expire = datetime.now(timezone.utc) + timedelta(hours=settings.auth_jwt_expire_hours)
    21→    payload = {"sub": subject, "exp": expire}
    22→    return jwt.encode(payload, settings.auth_jwt_secret, algorithm="HS256")
    23→
    24→
    25→async def get_current_user(token: str = Depends(oauth2_scheme)) -> str:
    26→    try:
    27→        payload = jwt.decode(token, settings.auth_jwt_secret, algorithms=["HS256"])
    28→        username: str | None = payload.get("sub")
    29→        if username is None:
    30→            raise HTTPException(status_code=status.HTTP_401_UNAUTHORIZED, detail="Ungültiger Token")
    31→    except JWTError:
    32→        raise HTTPException(status_code=status.HTTP_401_UNAUTHORIZED, detail="Ungültiger Token")
    33→    return username
    34→
    35→
    36→def verify_ws_token(token: str | None) -> str | None:
    37→    """Validate a token string from a WebSocket query parameter.
    38→
    39→    Returns the username on success, None on failure.
    40→    """
    41→    if not token:
    42→        return None
    43→    try:
    44→        payload = jwt.decode(token, settings.auth_jwt_secret, algorithms=["HS256"])
    45→        return payload.get("sub")
    46→    except JWTError:
    47→        return None
    48→

     1→"""FastAPI application entry point."""
     2→
     3→import sys
     4→from contextlib import asynccontextmanager
     5→from pathlib import Path
     6→
     7→from fastapi import Depends, FastAPI
     8→from fastapi.middleware.cors import CORSMiddleware
     9→from fastapi.staticfiles import StaticFiles
    10→
    11→from .auth import get_current_user
    12→from .config import settings
    13→from .database import init_db
    14→
    15→# Add bandwacht module to path
    16→sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent.parent))
    17→
    18→
    19→@asynccontextmanager
    20→async def lifespan(app: FastAPI):
    21→    await init_db()
    22→    yield
    23→    # Shutdown: stop all monitors
    24→    from .services.monitor_manager import manager
    25→    await manager.stop_all()
    26→
    27→
    28→app = FastAPI(title="BandWacht Web", version="0.1.0", lifespan=lifespan)
    29→
    30→app.add_middleware(
    31→    CORSMiddleware,
    32→    allow_origins=settings.cors_origin_list,
    33→    allow_credentials=True,
    34→    allow_methods=["*"],
    35→    allow_headers=["*"],
    36→)
    37→
    38→# Import and include routers
    39→from .routers import auth as auth_router  # noqa: E402
    40→from .routers import events, instances, notifications, recordings, settings as settings_router, targets  # noqa: E402
    41→from .services.ws_bridge import router as ws_router  # noqa: E402
    42→
    43→# Auth router (public)
    44→app.include_router(auth_router.router, prefix="/api/v1", tags=["auth"])
    45→
    46→# Protected routers — require valid JWT
    47→_auth = [Depends(get_current_user)]
    48→app.include_router(instances.router, prefix="/api/v1", tags=["instances"], dependencies=_auth)
    49→app.include_router(targets.router, prefix="/api/v1", tags=["targets"], dependencies=_auth)
    50→app.include_router(events.router, prefix="/api/v1", tags=["events"], dependencies=_auth)
    51→app.include_router(recordings.router, prefix="/api/v1", tags=["recordings"], dependencies=_auth)
    52→app.include_router(notifications.router, prefix="/api/v1", tags=["notifications"], dependencies=_auth)
    53→app.include_router(settings_router.router, prefix="/api/v1", tags=["settings"], dependencies=_auth)
    54→app.include_router(ws_router)
    55→
    56→
    57→@app.get("/api/v1/health")
    58→async def health():
    59→    from .services.monitor_manager import manager
    60→    running = sum(1 for t in manager._tasks.values() if not t.done())
    61→    return {"status": "ok", "version": "0.1.0", "monitors_running": running}
    62→
    63→
    64→# Serve built frontend as static files (production)
    65→frontend_dist = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent / "frontend" / "dist"
    66→if frontend_dist.is_dir():
    67→    app.mount("/", StaticFiles(directory=str(frontend_dist), html=True), name="frontend")
    68→

     1→"""WebSocket endpoints for live FFT and event streaming."""
     2→
     3→import asyncio
     4→import json
     5→import logging
     6→
     7→from fastapi import APIRouter, Query, WebSocket, WebSocketDisconnect
     8→
     9→from ..auth import verify_ws_token
    10→from .monitor_manager import manager
    11→
    12→logger = logging.getLogger("bandwacht.web.ws")
    13→router = APIRouter()
    14→
    15→
    16→@router.websocket("/ws/spectrum/{instance_id}")
    17→async def ws_spectrum(websocket: WebSocket, instance_id: int, token: str = Query(default="")):
    18→    """Stream live FFT data for an instance."""
    19→    user = verify_ws_token(token)
    20→    if not user:
    21→        await websocket.close(code=1008, reason="Nicht autorisiert")
    22→        return
    23→
    24→    await websocket.accept()
    25→    logger.info(f"WS spectrum client connected for instance {instance_id}")
    26→    queue: asyncio.Queue = asyncio.Queue(maxsize=5)
    27→    msg_count = 0
    28→
    29→    async def on_fft(fft_data: list):
    30→        try:
    31→            queue.put_nowait(fft_data)
    32→        except asyncio.QueueFull:
    33→            # Drop oldest, keep newest
    34→            try:
    35→                queue.get_nowait()
    36→            except asyncio.QueueEmpty:
    37→                pass
    38→            try:
    39→                queue.put_nowait(fft_data)
    40→            except asyncio.QueueFull:
    41→                pass
    42→
    43→    manager.subscribe_fft(instance_id, on_fft)
    44→    try:
    45→        while True:
    46→            fft_data = await queue.get()
    47→            await websocket.send_text(json.dumps(fft_data))
    48→            msg_count += 1
    49→            if msg_count == 1:
    50→                logger.info(f"WS spectrum: first FFT frame sent to client (instance {instance_id}, {len(fft_data)} bins)")
    51→    except (WebSocketDisconnect, Exception) as e:
    52→        logger.info(f"WS spectrum client disconnected for instance {instance_id} ({type(e).__name__}, sent {msg_count} frames)")
    53→    finally:
    54→        manager.unsubscribe_fft(instance_id, on_fft)
    55→
    56→
    57→@router.websocket("/ws/events")
    58→async def ws_events(websocket: WebSocket, token: str = Query(default="")):
    59→    """Stream live detection events globally."""
    60→    user = verify_ws_token(token)
    61→    if not user:
    62→        await websocket.close(code=1008, reason="Nicht autorisiert")
    63→        return
    64→
    65→    await websocket.accept()
    66→    logger.info("WS events client connected")
    67→    queue: asyncio.Queue = asyncio.Queue(maxsize=100)
    68→
    69→    async def on_event(event_data: dict):
    70→        try:
    71→            queue.put_nowait(event_data)
    72→        except asyncio.QueueFull:
    73→            pass
    74→
    75→    manager.subscribe_events(on_event)
    76→    try:
    77→        while True:
    78→            event_data = await queue.get()
    79→            await websocket.send_text(json.dumps(event_data))
    80→    except (WebSocketDisconnect, Exception) as e:
    81→        logger.info(f"WS events client disconnected ({type(e).__name__})")
    82→    finally:
    83→        manager.unsubscribe_events(on_event)
    84→

     1→const BASE = '/api/v1'
     2→
     3→const TOKEN_KEY = 'bandwacht_token'
     4→
     5→export function getToken(): string | null {
     6→  return localStorage.getItem(TOKEN_KEY)
     7→}
     8→
     9→export function setToken(token: string): void {
    10→  localStorage.setItem(TOKEN_KEY, token)
    11→}
    12→
    13→export function clearToken(): void {
    14→  localStorage.removeItem(TOKEN_KEY)
    15→}
    16→
    17→let onAuthError: (() => void) | null = null
    18→
    19→export function setOnAuthError(cb: () => void): void {
    20→  onAuthError = cb
    21→}
    22→
    23→async function request<T>(path: string, options?: RequestInit): Promise<T> {
    24→  const headers: Record<string, string> = { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }
    25→  const token = getToken()
    26→  if (token) {
    27→    headers['Authorization'] = `Bearer ${token}`
    28→  }
    29→  const res = await fetch(`${BASE}${path}`, {
    30→    headers: { ...headers, ...options?.headers },
    31→    ...options,
    32→  })
    33→  if (res.status === 401) {
    34→    clearToken()
    35→    onAuthError?.()
    36→    throw new Error('Nicht autorisiert')
    37→  }
    38→  if (!res.ok) {
    39→    const body = await res.json().catch(() => ({ detail: res.statusText }))
    40→    throw new Error(body.detail || `HTTP ${res.status}`)
    41→  }
    42→  return res.json()
    43→}
    44→
    45→// --- Auth ---
    46→
    47→export const auth = {
    48→  login: async (username: string, password: string): Promise<import('../types').TokenResponse> => {
    49→    const body = new URLSearchParams({ username, password, grant_type: 'password' })
    50→    const res = await fetch(`${BASE}/auth/login`, {
    51→      method: 'POST',
    52→      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/x-www-form-urlencoded' },
    53→      body,
    54→    })
    55→    if (!res.ok) {
    56→      const data = await res.json().catch(() => ({ detail: res.statusText }))
    57→      throw new Error(data.detail || `HTTP ${res.status}`)
    58→    }
    59→    const data: import('../types').TokenResponse = await res.json()
    60→    setToken(data.access_token)
    61→    return data
    62→  },
    63→  logout: () => {
    64→    clearToken()
    65→  },
    66→}
    67→
    68→// --- Instances ---
    69→
    70→export const instances = {
    71→  list: () => request<import('../types').SdrInstance[]>('/instances'),
    72→  get: (id: number) => request<import('../types').SdrInstance>(`/instances/${id}`),
    73→  create: (data: { name: string; url: string; enabled?: boolean; desired_profile?: string | null }) =>
    74→    request<import('../types').SdrInstance>('/instances', {
    75→      method: 'POST',
    76→      body: JSON.stringify(data),
    77→    }),
    78→  update: (id: number, data: Partial<{ name: string; url: string; enabled: boolean; desired_profile: string | null }>) =>
    79→    request<import('../types').SdrInstance>(`/instances/${id}`, {
    80→      method: 'PUT',
    81→      body: JSON.stringify(data),
    82→    }),
    83→  delete: (id: number) => request<{ ok: boolean }>(`/instances/${id}`, { method: 'DELETE' }),
    84→  start: (id: number) => request<{ ok: boolean }>(`/instances/${id}/start`, { method: 'POST' }),
    85→  stop: (id: number) => request<{ ok: boolean }>(`/instances/${id}/stop`, { method: 'POST' }),
    86→  status: (id: number) => request<any>(`/instances/${id}/status`),
    87→  profiles: (id: number) => request<import('../types').AvailableProfile[]>(`/instances/${id}/profiles`),
    88→}
    89→
    90→// --- Targets ---
    91→
    92→export const targets = {
    93→  list: (instanceId?: number) =>
    94→    request<import('../types').WatchTarget[]>(
    95→      `/targets${instanceId != null ? `?instance_id=${instanceId}` : ''}`
    96→    ),
    97→  create: (data: {
    98→    instance_id: number
    99→    freq_hz: number
   100→    bandwidth_hz?: number
   101→    label?: string
   102→    threshold_db?: number
   103→    enabled?: boolean
   104→  }) =>
   105→    request<import('../types').WatchTarget>('/targets', {
   106→      method: 'POST',
   107→      body: JSON.stringify(data),
   108→    }),
   109→  update: (id: number, data: Partial<import('../types').WatchTarget>) =>
   110→    request<import('../types').WatchTarget>(`/targets/${id}`, {
   111→      method: 'PUT',
   112→      body: JSON.stringify(data),
   113→    }),
   114→  delete: (id: number) => request<{ ok: boolean }>(`/targets/${id}`, { method: 'DELETE' }),
   115→}
   116→
   117→// --- Events ---
   118→
   119→export const events = {
   120→  list: (params?: {
   121→    page?: number
   122→    page_size?: number
   123→    instance_id?: number
   124→    freq_min?: number
   125→    freq_max?: number
   126→    date_from?: string
   127→    date_to?: string

   134→      })

   136→    const qs = searchParams.toString()

   138→      `/events${qs ? `?${qs}` : ''}`
   139→    )
   140→  },
   141→  stats: () => request<import('../types').EventStats>('/events/stats'),
   142→  exportCsv: () => `${BASE}/events/export`,
   143→}
   144→
   145→// --- Recordings ---
   146→
   147→export const recordings = {
   148→  list: () => request<string[]>('/recordings'),
   149→  url: (filename: string) => `${BASE}/recordings/${encodeURIComponent(filename)}`,
   150→  delete: (filename: string) =>
   151→    request<{ ok: boolean }>(`/recordings/${encodeURIComponent(filename)}`, { method: 'DELETE' }),
   152→}
   153→
   154→// --- Notifications ---
   155→
   156→export const notifications = {
   157→  list: () => request<import('../types').NotificationConfig[]>('/notifications'),
   158→  test: (backend: string) => request<{ ok: boolean }>(`/notifications/${backend}/test`, { method: 'POST' }),
   159→}
   160→
   161→// --- Settings ---

   163→export const settings = {
   164→  get: () => request<import('../types').GlobalSettings>('/settings'),
   165→  update: (data: Partial<import('../types').GlobalSettings>) =>
   166→    request<import('../types').GlobalSettings>('/settings', {
   167→      method: 'PUT',
   168→      body: JSON.stringify(data),
   169→    }),
   170→}
   171→
   172→// --- Health ---

   174→export const health = () => request<{ status: string; version: string }>('/health')
   175→
